L # Expand an `if_any()` or `if_all()` call
T 41 source.r comment.line
L #
T 1 source.r comment.line
L # Always guaranteed to be 1 quosure in, 1 quosure out, unlike `expand_across()`.
T 80 source.r comment.line
L #
T 1 source.r comment.line
L # For the dplyr backend, the main reason we expand at all is to evaluate
T 72 source.r comment.line
L # tidyselection exactly once (rather than once per group), because tidyselection
T 80 source.r comment.line
L # is rather slow.
T 17 source.r comment.line
L #
T 1 source.r comment.line
L # At one point we believed `if_any()` and `if_all()` could be implemented as
T 76 source.r comment.line
L # "pure expansion" that would run before dispatching to other backends, like
T 76 source.r comment.line
L # dbplyr. In theory this could expand to a chain of `&` and `|` operations that
T 79 source.r comment.line
L # dbplyr would already know how to translate (so dbplyr itself would not have to
T 80 source.r comment.line
L # know how to implement `if_any()` and `if_all()`), but in practice we need more
T 80 source.r comment.line
L # error checking than what `x & y & z` gets us, so we actually expand to a
T 74 source.r comment.line
L # vctrs-backed implementation since the "pure expansion" ideas have never played
T 80 source.r comment.line
L # out.
T 6 source.r comment.line

T 1 source.r comment.line
L # We detect and mark inlinable lambdas here. By lambda we mean either
T 69 source.r comment.line
L # a `~` or `function` call that is directly supplied to
T 55 source.r comment.line
L # `across()`. Lambdas haven't been evaluated yet and don't carry an
T 67 source.r comment.line
L # environment.
T 14 source.r comment.line
L #
T 1 source.r comment.line
L # Inlinable lambdas are eventually expanded in the surrounding call.
T 68 source.r comment.line
L # To distinguish inlinable lambdas from non-inlinable ones, we set
T 66 source.r comment.line
L # their environments to the empty env.
T 38 source.r comment.line
L #
T 1 source.r comment.line
L # There are cases where we can't inline, for instance lambdas that are
T 70 source.r comment.line
L # passed additional arguments through `...`. We still want these
T 64 source.r comment.line
L # non-inlinable lambdas to be maskable so that they can refer to
T 64 source.r comment.line
L # data-mask columns. So we set them (a) in the evaluation case, to
T 66 source.r comment.line
L # their original quosure environment which is the data mask, or (b) in
T 70 source.r comment.line
L # the expansion case, to the uninitialised data mask.
T 53 source.r comment.line
L #
T 1 source.r comment.line
L # @value  <fn> | <list<fn>>. Inlinable lambdas are set to the
T 61 source.r comment.line
